@@COMMIT@@	5b5a3a389de97a5eb84fe8adfda85136350ce636	9d75d69289b6ae8616abd91fea7b70829ba02f94	2026-03-06T17:01:54-06:00	2026-03-06T17:01:54-06:00	HanzoRazer	refs/tags/v2.4.0-alpha.1	feat(vectorizer): add SIMPLE extraction mode for non-guitar instrumentsAdd ExtractionMode enum with SMART (default) and SIMPLE options:

- SMART: ML-based classification, optimized for guitars (existing behavior)
- SIMPLE: Raw contour extraction without filtering - works for any instrument

SIMPLE mode bypasses ML classification and extracts ALL contours above
min_area threshold using adaptive thresholding. Works for cuatros,
mandolins, and other instruments that the guitar-trained classifier
may filter out as "noise".

Usage:
  # Via constructor
  v = Phase3Vectorizer(extraction_mode=ExtractionMode.SIMPLE)

  # Via extract() override
  v.extract(source, output, extraction_mode=ExtractionMode.SIMPLE)

Tested on Cuatro blueprints: 500-600 contours vs ~60 with SMART mode.

- Expand ACOUSTIC_FEATURES with archtop/Selmer-specific features:
  - D_HOLE: D-shaped soundhole (Selmer/Maccaferri)
  - F_HOLE: Archtop f-holes
  - NECK_PROFILE: Neck cross-sections
  - TAILPIECE: Selmer/archtop tailpiece
  - BRIDGE: Acoustic bridge
  - ROSETTE: Decorative rosette ring
  - HEEL_BLOCK: Neck heel block

- Add non-strict classification mode (default):
  - Uses broad size categories: BODY, LARGE, MEDIUM, SMALL, DETAIL
  - Captures ALL features without mutual exclusion
  - Prevents features from being missed due to overlapping size ranges

- Add strict_classification parameter to _extract_guitar_features()
  - strict=False (default): broad categories, captures everything
  - strict=True: attempts specific feature classification

- dark_threshold: line detection sensitivity
